[\[148b.3\]]brgya stong phrag mang por yang 'jig rten du de bzhin gshegs pa mthong yang srid mi mthong yang srid de[#]/_dper na u dum+WA [#]ra'i me tog bzhin no zhes gsungs so/_/rnam par gtan la dbab pa'i rgyal po'i mdo las 'byung ba/_kun dga' bo 'di lta ste/_u dum+WA [#]ra'i me tog ni sangs

[\[214b.3\]]med dang /_khye'u dang /_bu mo rnams kyi mdun na gnas par mthong yang zla ba'i dkyil 'khor mi rtog rnam par mi rtog ste/_'on kyang de lhun gyis grub pas/_rnam par mi rtog bzhin du ma 'dres pa'i chos dang ldan pas bya ba 'di lta bu 'byung ngo /_/de bzhin du bsam pa ji lta ba dang /_'dul ba ji 
[\[214b.4\]]lta ba bzhin du sems can de dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa mdun na bzhugs par [#]mthong yang /_de bzhin gshegs pa rnams mi rtog rnam par mi rtog ste/_'on kyang lhun gyis grub pas rnam par mi rtog bzhin du/_sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa dang ldan pas mdzad pa 'di lta bu 'byung ngo /_/
[\[214b.5\]]zhes rnam pa mang du gsungs so/_/mdo sde zab mo rgya chen po gang dag las/_sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i che ba nyid bstan pa de dag la mos pa dang /_yi ger 'dri [#]ba dang /_klog pa dang /_mchod pa dang /_bsti stang byed pa yang bsod nams shin tu [#]rgyas par 'phel bar 'gyur 
[\[214b.6\]]te mdo 'di nyid las/_'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na/_ri'i rgyal po ri rab ni 'di lta ste/_'phang dang /_rngams dang /_che ba nyid kyis ri thams cad zil gyis mnan nas/_lhag ge/_thal le/_lam me'o/_/'jam dpal de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa'i [#]chos kyi rnam grangs 'di la mos 
[\[214b.7\]]pas kyang /_'di lta ste mthong ba dang /_yangs [#]pa dang /_che ba nyid kyis mdo sde 'di la mos pa/_pha rol tu phyin pa lnga [#]las byung ba'i dge ba'i rtsa ba bskal pa grangs med par bsags pa'i byang chub sems dpas [#]phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rdul snyed zil gyis mnan nas/_lham me 
[\[215a\]]
[\[215a.1\]]dang [#]/_lhan ne dang [#]/_lhang nge'o/_/'jam dpal rigs kyi bu'am/_rigs kyi bu mo gang la las phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams kun gyi sems can thams cad byang chub kyi sems la rab tu bkod pa bas/_'jam dpal rigs kyi bu'am/_rigs

[\[215a.3\]]gzhan gang zhig gis/_chos kyi rnam grangs 'di la mos nas yi ger 'dri [#]ba'am tha na glegs bam la bris te/_mos pas 'chang ngam/_mar me dang /_bdug pa dang /_me tog dang /_spos dang /_phreng ba dang /_byug pas mchod na 'di ni de bas bsod nams ches grangs med pa
